# [Feat] mem_cache: Add programmable KV cache control and composable policies

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/46542) · state: open · merged: False

## Original PR description

## Motivation

Agentic workloads need to identify useful task prefixes during tool waits, move cached state between tiers, and restore it before a continuation is admitted. This implements the initial single-owner profile of [RFC #45189: Programmable KV Cache: Composable Policies for Agentic Serving](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/issues/45189).

Programmable control is disabled by default. Enabling it executes explicit commands; local policies require separate configuration.

## Modifications

- Add versioned native priority, copy, move, task-association, task-finish and signal actions through request-attached hints, HTTP, typed scheduler IPC and Python Engine APIs.
- Resolve token/content/session targets against immutable history. Track bounded replay/results, task incarnations and independent cancellation interests; bind `request_cached` only after successful request completion and cache checkpointing.
- Apply copy-scoped soft priorities with independent revisions and TTLs. Explicit claims precede local-policy claims and session eviction preferences. Hits do not renew TTLs, eviction ends claims, and active request/transfer references remain protected.
- Execute FULL-prefix HiCache transfers across GPU, persistent host memory and file storage. Publish readiness after DMA/backend completion, drain worker access during cancellation, and reclaim MOVE sources only after destination publication and eligibility checks.
- Compose optional retention, borrowable quota and cost-aware tool-wait policies. Restored prefixes retain internal references until native prefill admission takes its request lock; allocation checks preserve decode headroom and account for shared prefixes.
- Isolate ordinary and control lookups by model/weight namespace, including guarded weight transitions and cache resets.
- Keep a concise API/configuration README, six focused CPU test modules and one registered CUDA round-trip test.

The supported serving profile has one TP/PP/CP/DP owner and ordinary text requests. Priority control supports FULL/SWA/Mamba through the Python tree backend. Movement requires FULL attention, persistent-host HiCache and `--disable-overlap-schedule`; file storage is optional. Remote-owner transfers, direct linkers, speculative decoding, PD disaggregation, LoRA and multimodal control are unsupported.

## Accuracy Tests

- CPU regression selection: **314 passed**, including existing prefill, OpenAI request conversion and communicator tests.
- All six new CPU modules also execute through the repository CI runner's `python3 FILE -f` entry point.
- Incremental statement coverage across modified runtime Python files: **82.35%** against the branch's main baseline.
- CUDA test on one A100: GPU → Host/File MOVE, poisoned device buffers, real restore, exact K/V values, preparation-to-request lock handoff and complete allocator reclamation. Both Host and file paths pass.

Pretrained-model answer/logit comparisons remain pending.

## Speed Tests and Profiling

Serving performance benchmarks remain pending. The CUDA test validates transfer correctness and resource ownership; it does not establish throughput or latency improvements.

## Checklist

- [x] Format changed files with the repository pre-commit configuration.
- [x] Add focused tests using `CustomTestCase`, CI registration and standard test entry points.
- [x] Document configuration, API semantics and supported scope.
- [ ] Provide pretrained-model accuracy and representative serving benchmark results.
- [x] Follow the SGLang code style guidance.
